A. STABLE IDENTITY AND SOURCE EVIDENCE
Audit order: 141
Batch: BATCH_15
Requirement ID: IMO-111
Source family: IMO
Verification mode: DIRECT_STATIC
Source document: International Code for Ships Operating in Polar Waters (Polar Code)
Source revision: Resolution MSC.385(94), adopted 21 Nov 2014; consolidated with MEPC.264(68), adopted 15 May 2015
Clause / section / page: 1.2.4 / Part II-A Chapter 1 - Structural requirements / 41
Source URL: https://wwwcdn.imo.org/localresources/en/KnowledgeCentre/IndexofIMOResolutions/MSCResolutions/MSC.385%2894%29.pdf
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/MSC.385(94).pdf
Local source PDF SHA-256: 6c6a038bff68ee906dd97c41e95fb4442917c014c8e79228f15e80cc5afa5c1f
Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-IMO-111-20260915T113542821113Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
For category A and B ships constructed on or after 1 January 2017 all oil residue (sludge) tanks and oily bilge water holding tanks shall be separated from the outer shell by a distance of not less than 0.76 m. This provision does not apply to small tanks with a maximum individual capacity not greater than 30 m^3.
Normalized requirement:
IF ship_category IN {'A','B'} AND construction_date >= '2017-01-01' AND tank_type IN {oil_residue_tank, oily_bilge_water_holding_tank} AND individual_tank_capacity_m3 > 30, THEN tank_outer_shell_separation_m >= 0.76.
Required inputs: Ship category/date and tank inventory/capacity/separation measurements

Locked context, definitions, ownership, units, and dependency contract:

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:IMO_111_ShipShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipCategory ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:in ( nltl:polarShipCategoryA nltl:polarShipCategoryB nltl:polarShipCategoryC ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship category must be Category A, B, or C."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:shipCategory ;
                sh:in ( nltl:polarShipCategoryA nltl:polarShipCategoryB ) ;
                sh:minCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:constructionDate ;
                sh:datatype xsd:date ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:message "Category A or B ships must have a construction date."
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:shipCategory ;
                sh:hasValue nltl:polarShipCategoryC ;
                sh:minCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "For a Category A or B ship constructed on or after 1 January 2017, each specified oil residue or oily bilge water holding tank with capacity greater than 30 m3 must have an outer-shell separation of at least 0.76 m." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT DISTINCT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:shipCategory ?category ;
                      nltl:constructionDate ?constructionDate ;
                      nltl:hasComponent ?tank .

                FILTER (
                    ?category IN (
                        nltl:polarShipCategoryA,
                        nltl:polarShipCategoryB
                    )
                )
                FILTER (?constructionDate >= "2017-01-01"^^xsd:date)

                ?tank nltl:tankType ?tankType .
                FILTER (
                    EXISTS {
                        ?tank a nltl:oilResidueTank .
                    }
                    ||
                    EXISTS {
                        ?tank a nltl:oilyBilgeWaterHoldingTank .
                    }
                )

                ?tank nltl:individualTankCapacity ?capacity .
                ?capacity qudt:numericValue ?capacityValue ;
                          qudt:unit unit:M3 .

                FILTER (?capacityValue > "30"^^xsd:decimal)

                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    ?tank nltl:tankOuterShellSeparation ?separation .
                    ?separation qudt:numericValue ?separationValue ;
                                 qudt:unit unit:M .

                    FILTER (?separationValue >= "0.76"^^xsd:decimal)
                }
            }
        """
    ] .

```

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .

@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:IMO_111_Shape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipCategory ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:polarShipCategoryValue
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:tankType ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:individualTankCapacity ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M3 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:tankOuterShellSeparation ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Category A or B ships constructed on or after 1 January 2017 require at least 0.76 m separation for applicable tanks with capacity greater than 30 m3." ;
        sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

SELECT $this
WHERE {
    $this nltl:shipCategory ?category ;
          nltl:constructionDate ?constructionDate ;
          nltl:tankType ?tankType ;
          nltl:individualTankCapacity ?capacity .

    ?capacity qudt:numericValue ?capacityValue ;
              qudt:unit unit:M3 .

    FILTER (
        ?category IN (
            nltl:polarShipCategoryA,
            nltl:polarShipCategoryB
        )
    )
    FILTER (?constructionDate >= "2017-01-01"^^xsd:date)
    FILTER (
        ?tankType = "oil_residue_tank" ||
        ?tankType = "oily_bilge_water_holding_tank"
    )
    FILTER (?capacityValue > "30"^^xsd:decimal)

        ?separation qudt:numericValue ?separationValue ;
```

The design ice pressure is determined by the formula p = c_d*c_p*c_a*p_0 [MPa] (4.1), where c_d is a factor which takes account of the influence of the size and engine output of the ship. The maximum value is c_d = 1. It is calculated using c_d = (a*k + b)/1000 (4.2).

```turtle
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:nominalIcePressureP0 ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:MegaPA ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasDirectAnalysisCase ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class nltl:directAnalysisCase ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:iceLoadAreaFactorCa ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipSizeEngineOutputFactorCd ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:icePressure ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:MegaPA ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The calculated ship size engine output factor and ice pressure must match the declared formula values." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:shipSizeEngineOutputCoefficientA ?aNode ;
                      nltl:continuousPropulsionPowerAvailableInIce ?mcrPowerNode ;
                      nltl:shipSizeEngineOutputCoefficientB ?bNode ;
                      nltl:iceClassFactorCp ?cpNode ;
                      nltl:nominalIcePressureP0 ?p0Node ;
                      nltl:shipSizeEngineOutputFactorCd ?cdNode ;
                      nltl:icePressure ?pNode ;
                      nltl:hasDirectAnalysisCase ?case .

                ?case nltl:iceLoadAreaFactorCa ?caNode .

                ?aNode qudt:numericValue ?a .
                ?mcrPowerNode qudt:numericValue ?mcrPower_kW .
                ?bNode qudt:numericValue ?b .
                ?cpNode qudt:numericValue ?cp .
                ?caNode qudt:numericValue ?ca .
                ?p0Node qudt:numericValue ?p0 .
                ?cdNode qudt:numericValue ?reportedCd .
                ?pNode qudt:numericValue ?reportedP .

                BIND((?a * ?mcrPower_kW + ?b) / 1000 AS ?rawCd)
                BIND(IF(?rawCd > 1, 1, ?rawCd) AS ?expectedCd)
                BIND(?expectedCd * ?cp * ?ca * ?p0 AS ?expectedP)

                FILTER(
                    ABS(?reportedCd - ?expectedCd) >
                    (0.000001 * ABS(?expectedCd) + 0.000000001)
                    ||
                    ABS(?reportedP - ?expectedP) >
                    (0.000001 * ABS(?expectedP) + 0.000000001)
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

```

            ]
        ]
```turtle
    ] .
```

Original source text:
For category A and B oil tankers of less than 5,000 tonnes deadweight constructed on or after 1 January 2017, the entire cargo tank length shall be protected with: double bottom tanks or spaces complying with the applicable requirements of regulation 19.6.1 of MARPOL Annex I; and wing tanks or spaces arranged in accordance with regulation 19.3.1 of MARPOL Annex I and complying with the applicable requirements for distance referred to in regulation 19.6.2 of MARPOL Annex I.
Normalized requirement:
IF ship_category IN {'A','B'} AND ship_type = oil_tanker AND deadweight_tonnes < 5000 AND construction_date >= '2017-01-01', THEN entire_cargo_tank_length_double_bottom_protection_status = compliant with MARPOL I/19.6.1 AND wing_tank_protection_status = compliant with MARPOL I/19.3.1 and I/19.6.2.
Required inputs: Ship particulars, protection arrangement, and linked MARPOL compliance evidence

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:IMO_110_StaticShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipCategory ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:in ( nltl:polarShipCategoryA nltl:polarShipCategoryB )
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipType ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:deadweight ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:TON_Metric ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:entireCargoTankLengthDoubleBottomProtectionStatus ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:wingTankProtectionStatus ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "For a Category A or B oil tanker of less than 5,000 tonnes deadweight constructed on or after 1 January 2017, both required cargo-tank protection compliance statuses must be true." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:shipCategory ?category ;
                      nltl:shipType ?shipType ;
                      nltl:constructionDate ?constructionDate ;
                      nltl:deadweight ?deadweight ;
                      a nltl:oilTanker .

                ?deadweight qudt:numericValue ?deadweightValue ;
                            qudt:unit unit:TON_Metric .

                FILTER (
                    ?category IN (nltl:polarShipCategoryA, nltl:polarShipCategoryB)
                    && ?shipType = "oil_tanker"
                    && ?deadweightValue < 5000
                    && YEAR(?constructionDate) >= 2017
                    && MONTH(?constructionDate) >= 1
                    && DAY(?constructionDate) >= 1
                )

                FILTER (
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:entireCargoTankLengthDoubleBottomProtectionStatus true
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:wingTankProtectionStatus true
                    }
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:IMO_110_ShipShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipCategory ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:in ( nltl:polarShipCategoryA nltl:polarShipCategoryB ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship category must be Category A or Category B."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipType ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship type is required to determine applicability."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The construction date is required to determine applicability."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:deadweight ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:TON_Metric ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "Deadweight is required as a metric-tonne quantity."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:entireCargoTankLengthDoubleBottomProtectionStatus ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:wingTankProtectionStatus ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "For an applicable Category A or B oil tanker constructed on or after 1 January 2017 with deadweight below 5,000 tonnes, both required cargo-tank protection statuses must be true." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:shipCategory ?category ;
                      nltl:shipType ?shipType ;
                      nltl:deadweight ?deadweight ;
                      nltl:constructionDate ?constructionDate .

                ?deadweight qudt:numericValue ?mass ;
                            qudt:unit unit:TON_Metric .

                FILTER (
                    ?category IN (
                        nltl:polarShipCategoryA,
                        nltl:polarShipCategoryB
                    )
                )
                FILTER (STR(?shipType) = "oil_tanker")
                FILTER (?mass < "5000"^^xsd:decimal)
                FILTER (
                    YEAR(?constructionDate) > 2017 ||
                    (
                        YEAR(?constructionDate) = 2017 &&
                        (
                            MONTH(?constructionDate) > 1 ||
                            (
                                MONTH(?constructionDate) = 1 &&
                                DAY(?constructionDate) >= 1
                            )
                        )
                    )
                )
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:entireCargoTankLengthDoubleBottomProtectionStatus true
                }
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:wingTankProtectionStatus true
                }
            }
        """
    ] .

```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .

gen:IMO_100_VoyagePlanHazardCoverageShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:voyagePlanPresent ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "The ship must record that a voyage plan is present."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:identifiedIntendedVoyageHazard ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:voyagePlanHazardCoverage ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Every identified intended voyage hazard must be addressed by the voyage plan." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:identifiedIntendedVoyageHazard ?hazard .
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:voyagePlanHazardCoverage true .
                }
            }
        """
    ] .
```

Q2 Ownership / paths: Are target ownership and property paths correct?
Q3 Required constraints: Are all mandatory obligations represented?
Q4 Units / calculation: Are quantities, units, formulae, and comparisons correct?

Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/TRAFICOM.pdf

Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-TRF-047-20260915T130504862305Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
The lower end of the strengthened part of a main frame and of an intermediate ice frame shall be attached to a deck, top or bottom plating of a tank, tank top or an ice stringer (section 4.5). Where an intermediate frame terminates below a deck, top or bottom plating of a tank, tank top or ice stringer which is situated at or below the lower limit of the ice belt (section 4.3.1), the lower end may be connected to the adjacent main frames by a horizontal member of the same scantlings as the main frames. Note that the main frames below the lower edge of the ice belt must be ice strengthened, see 4.4.1.
Normalized requirement:
The lower end of the strengthened part of a main frame and of an intermediate ice frame shall be attached to a deck, top or bottom plating of a tank, tank top or an ice stringer (section 4.5). Note that the main frames below the lower edge of the ice belt must be ice strengthened, see 4.4.1.

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gs: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gs:TRF047FrameShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:frame ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "An applicable main frame or intermediate ice frame must have a strengthened part whose lower end satisfies the permitted attachment or intermediate-frame exception." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                {
                    $this a nltl:intermediateIceFrame .
                }
                UNION
                {
                    $this a nltl:mainFrame ;
                          nltl:mainFrameBelowIceBeltStrengthened true .
                }

                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:hasStrengthenedPart ?strengthenedPart .
                    ?strengthenedPart nltl:hasLowerEnd ?lowerEnd .

                    {
                        ?lowerEnd nltl:hasAttachedSupportingStructure ?attachedStructure .
                        ?attachedStructure a ?attachedStructureKind .
                        FILTER (
                            ?attachedStructureKind IN (
                                nltl:deckStructure,
                                nltl:tankBoundaryPlating,
                                nltl:tankTop,
                                nltl:iceStringer
                            )
                        )
                    }
                    UNION
                    {
                        $this a nltl:intermediateIceFrame .
                        ?lowerEnd nltl:hasTerminationBelowSupportingStructure ?supportingStructure .
                        ?supportingStructure a ?supportingStructureKind ;
                                             nltl:supportingStructureAtOrBelowIceBeltLowerLimit true .
                        FILTER (
                            ?supportingStructureKind IN (
                                nltl:deckStructure,
                                nltl:tankBoundaryPlating,
                                nltl:tankTop,
                                nltl:iceStringer
                            )
                        )
                        ?lowerEnd nltl:hasHorizontalConnectionMember ?horizontalMember .
                        ?horizontalMember a nltl:horizontalConnectionMember ;
                                          nltl:connectsToAdjacentMainFrame ?adjacentMainFrame ;
                                          nltl:sameScantlingsAsMainFrame true .
                        ?adjacentMainFrame a nltl:mainFrame .
                    }
                }
            }
        """
    ] .

gs:TRF047MainFrameShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:mainFrame ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:mainFrameBelowIceBeltStrengthened ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The below-ice-belt strengthening applicability indicator must be a single boolean value."
    ] .

gs:TRF047TransverseFrameShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:transverseFrame .

gs:TRF047FrameEndShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:frameEnd .

gs:TRF047HorizontalConnectionMemberShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:horizontalConnectionMember ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:connectsToAdjacentMainFrame ;
        sh:class nltl:mainFrame ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A horizontal connection member must connect to an adjacent main frame."
    ] .
```

Q3 Required constraints: Are all mandatory obligations represented?

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gs: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gs:ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasCalculationCase ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:calculationCase ;
        sh:node gs:CalculationCaseShape ;
        sh:message "Each represented calculation case must follow the prescribed analytical procedure."
    ] .

gs:CalculationCaseShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:calculationCaseAssessedHullStructure ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:hullStructure ;
        sh:node gs:HullStructureShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A calculation case must identify its assessed hull structure."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:calculationMethod ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:calculationCaseAssessedHullStructure ;
                sh:node gs:ApplicableHullStructureShape
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:calculationMethod ;
                sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
                sh:in ( nltl:prescribedAnalyticalProcedure ) ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:message "Direct calculation must not replace the prescribed analytical procedure for shell-plating or local-frame requirements governed by I2.4, I2.6, or I2.7."
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:calculationCaseAssessedHullStructure ;
                sh:not [
                    sh:node gs:ApplicableHullStructureShape
                ]
            ]
        ]
    ) .

gs:ApplicableHullStructureShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:class nltl:plating
        ]
        [
            sh:class nltl:structuralMember
        ]
    ) .

gs:HullStructureShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:thickness ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
        sh:node gs:ThicknessQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The assessed hull structure must provide its thickness."
    ] .

gs:ThicknessQuantityShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
```
